	<div class="container mt-lg-5 pt-lg-5">
		<div class="row mt-lg-5 pt-lg-5">
			<div class="col-12">
				<div class="card bg-color-chartreuse px-2 px-lg-4 py-3 mx-4 mx-md-auto wow fadeInRight">
					<div class="card-header-wrap">
						<h2 class="font-size-capitalised color-eden d-block text-center">
							04 / Nature Reserves						</h2>
						<hr/>
					</div>
					<div class="card-content-wrap px-2 px-md-5 pb-4 pb-lg-5">
						<h3 class="display-1 h1 color-eden pt-lg-4 mt-5 mb-4 mb-lg-5">
							Our Nature Reserves						</h3>
						<p>A Nature Reserve is a protected area of importance to wildlife. Ireland has 77 registered nature reserves that all play an integral role in preserving nature for future generations. Most are owned by the State, but some are owned by organisations or private landowners.</p>
						<div class="mb-5"></div>
					</div>	
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
	<div class="section-card-extra-element"></div>
</section>

<section id="section-text-scroll-block_2acb02b7803b0ae67dac0e1f87f3dcd6" class="section-text-scroll bg-color-grass-green color-white py-5 bg-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/micro-IMG_4444.jpg);  background-size:cover;">
		<div class="text-scroll-box py-lg-5">
		<div class="container py-3 py-lg-5">
			<div class="row">
				<div class="col-12">
					<div class="text-scroll-box-main px-3 py-2 mx-auto">

					<div class="text-scroll-box-scroll color-white h3 px-3 py-2 mx-auto wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.15s">
						<div class="container">
<div class="row my-5 py-5">
<div class="col-4 col-lg-6 py-5">
			<img decoding="async" class="d-table ms-auto img-fluid w-100 me-lg-4" src="/app/themes/NationalParks/resources/assets/images/imagenow/Icons/PNG/National Parks_Web Icons__84.png" style="max-width:200px;" alt="National Parks">
		</div>

<h2 class="display-1 text-center color-white mb-0" style="line-height:1;">Protect <br />Nature</h2>
</p></div>

</p></div>

					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
	</section>


<section id="protect-nature" class="section-infobox bg-color-cyprus pb-5">
	<h2 class="font-size-capitalised color-white d-block text-center pt-4">05 / Protect Nature</h2>
	<hr class="bg-color-white px-3 px-lg-5"/>
	<div class="container">
		<div class="row">
			<div class="col-12 col-md-8 offset-md-2 my-5 py-5 color-white font-size-quote text-center">
				<p>National Parks inspire visitors to create their own experiences and journeys, where they feel rejuvenated and restored, with a greater understanding of conservation and care.</p>
<p>Now, more than ever, we must listen to this voice — nature’s voice — in our National Parks.</p>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
	<div class="container-fluid infobox-grid px-lg-5">			
		<div class="row">
								
				<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 col-xl-3">
					<div class="px-4 py-4 py-lg-5">
						<div class="row">
							<div class="col-4">
								<img decoding="async" class="w-100" src="https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/National-Parks_Web-Icons__72.png" alt="" />
							</div>
							<div class="col-8 color-white">
								<p class="mb-0 ps-3">01 / Think about the best time to visit and how you’ll travel here, to minimise your environmental impact.</p>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
					
				<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 col-xl-3">
					<div class="px-4 py-4 py-lg-5">
						<div class="row">
							<div class="col-4">
								<img decoding="async" class="w-100" src="https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/National-Parks_Web-Icons__71.png" alt="" />
							</div>
							<div class="col-8 color-white">
								<p class="mb-0 ps-3">02 / Respect wildlife and always keep your dogs on a lead.</p>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
					
				<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 col-xl-3">
					<div class="px-4 py-4 py-lg-5">
						<div class="row">
							<div class="col-4">
								<img decoding="async" class="w-100" src="https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/National-Parks_Web-Icons__73.png" alt="" />
							</div>
							<div class="col-8 color-white">
								<p class="mb-0 ps-3">03 / Leave what you find and do not disturb the flora and fauna.</p>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
					
				<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 col-xl-3">
					<div class="px-4 py-4 py-lg-5">
						<div class="row">
							<div class="col-4">
								<img decoding="async" class="w-100" src="https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/National-Parks_Web-Icons__74.png" alt="" />
							</div>
							<div class="col-8 color-white">
								<p class="mb-0 ps-3">04 / Dispose of waste properly, litter is a huge threat to nature.</p>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
									</div>
	</div>
</section>

<section id="section-allparks-slideup-infoboxes-block_089dc8090d3c0fb8ff73d5eb3d8a9746" class="section-allparks-slideup-infoboxes bg-color-tea-light px-3 px-sm-5 pt-3 pt-sm-5 pb-0">
	<div class="container p-0">
			<ul class="row flex-column flex-md-row align-items-end p-0 mb-0" tabindex="0" role="list" aria-label="Habitats details">
															
					<li class="slide-info col-12 col-lg-6">
						<div id="collapse1" class="slide-collapse d-flex flex-column bg-color-tea-light" aria-labelledby="heading1">
						<button class="slide-button px-4 pt-2 sb-1" type="button" data-target="#collapse1" data-counter="1" aria-expanded="true" aria-controls="collapse1">
								Habitats							</button>
						<ul class="slide-body flex-grow-1 p-0" tabindex="0" role="list" aria-label="Habitats">
																								
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Bog</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Blanket bogs form in mountain areas and follow the contours of the hills. Raised bogs grow as large domes, mainly in the midlands. Both are a patchwork of raised drier hummocks and wetter pools and boggy patches.</p>
<p>Typical bog plants include mosses, especially Sphagnum, and heathers, bilberry, crowberry, sedges and cotton grass, and distinctive lichens. </p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Woodland</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image-1.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>There are various woodland habitats present in Ireland such as Deciduous Woodland, which can contain excellent examples of native species such as ash, hazel, pine and oak.</p>
<p>These woodlands often contain other native tree species also; wych elm, spindle, downy birch, holly, willow, crab apple, rowan, yew and aspen.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Lakes &#038; Ponds</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image-3.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Lakes and ponds are enclosed bodies of freshwater, varying in size from small pools out on the open bog to large lakes which have formed high in the hollows of mountain corries. Corries are hollows found on mountainsides which were carved out by the action of glaciers during the last ice age. The plants and wildlife in lakes and ponds vary with the surrounding habitats and geology.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Calcareous Grassland</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Mountain-Sorrel.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Calcareous grassland is restricted in its distribution and is now largely confined to the steep slopes of esker ridges and moraines in the midlands. It is found on the terraces of the mountains and between the limestone pavement, as well as on glacial deposits throughout the Burren. These calcareous grasslands host an extraordinary composition of flora, and attract botanists from all over the world to study this unique mixture of plants growing together.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Limestone Pavement</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image-7.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Limestone pavement has become synonymous with the Burren and covers most of the National Park, although usually in a mosaic with other habitats. The pavement may be of either a smooth or shattered type.</p>
<p>The smooth limestone pavement areas consist of clints and grykes. The clints are the slab-like flat surface areas of the pavement and the grykes are the fissured cracks that dissect the clints. The grykes provide shelter for the soil to accumulate and also shelter from the wind.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Heath</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image-4.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Wet heaths are botanically similar to blanket bogs. They can occur on varied types of terrain, but are best represented on moderately steep, badly drained moraine slopes, where the peat is rarely much more than one metre deep. Typical species include ling heather, cross-leaved heath, gorse and bog myrtle, with purple moor-grass, deer-grass, cotton-grass, tormentil, heath rush and some bog moss species.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																							</ul>
						</div>
						<div class="slideup-infobox-header accordion-header" id="heading1">
							<div class="header-image" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/56ab07bd0d8df6b940f4c3551fc188ee-scaled.jpg);"></div>
							<button class="slide-button collapsed sb-1 px-4 pt-2" type="button" data-counter="1" data-target="#collapse1" aria-expanded="true" aria-controls="collapse1">
								Habitats							</button>
						</div>
					</li>
											
					<li class="slide-info col-12 col-lg-6">
						<div id="collapse2" class="slide-collapse d-flex flex-column bg-color-tea-light" aria-labelledby="heading2">
						<button class="slide-button px-4 pt-2 sb-2" type="button" data-target="#collapse2" data-counter="2" aria-expanded="true" aria-controls="collapse2">
								Species							</button>
						<ul class="slide-body flex-grow-1 p-0" tabindex="0" role="list" aria-label="Habitats">
																								
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Flora</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image-2.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>There are many interesting species of note to see in Ireland’s National Parks such as the beautiful and varied orchids that grow in the Burren. Species such as the early-purple orchid, bee orchid, butterfly orchid and fly orchid, among others. There are also some rare species to be found in Wild Nephin National Park such as marsh saxifrage; a very rare yellow flower which grows in nutrient rich wet flushes; and ivy-leaved bellflower which grows along the riverbanks of the National Park.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Trees</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Image.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Glenveagh National Park is home to a mix of native and non-native trees. The main species are sessile oak, downy birch, rowan, holly, hazel, yew and aspen. Native scots pines are also a key feature having been planted as shelter belts around the lake shore. Up to 2,000 seedlings are to be planted as one of the initial steps in the development of an acorn-to-tree approach to woodland conservation management within the park.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Mammals</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Large-Header-Image-1.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Some of Ireland’s native mammal species include the red deer, red squirrel, red fox and Irish hare. Native breeds include Kerry cow, Galway sheep and Connemara pony.</p>
<p>The red deer herd found in Killarney National Park dates back to Neolithic times and is now the only surviving indigenous herd left in the country. While herds of red deer can be found elsewhere in Ireland, they are from re-introduced stock (mainly from Scotland).</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Birds</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Large-Header-Image.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>The birdlife that can be spotted in Ireland is plentiful, including bird species of interest; such as the merlin, goosander, whinchat and red grouse. Peregrine falcons are the world’s fastest birds, and are a notable species in Wicklow Mountains National Park. White-tailed sea eagles hadn’t been seen in Ireland since the early 20th century until a reintroduction programme in Killarney National Park, and now there are white-tailed sea eagles in the sky once more.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Insects</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image-5.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>There are many magnificant species of butterfly present in Ireland such as the protected marsh fritillary. As the name suggests, the marsh fritillary likes wet or marshy ground and relies on the devil’s bit scabious plant for its lifecycle.<br />
The Burren National Park is rich in butterfly species; in fact, the area around Mullaghmore is considered to be the richest butterfly site in Ireland. Twenty-seven of Ireland’s 33 resident and regular migrant butterflies have been recorded here.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																	
								<li class="">
									<div class="row">
										<div class="col-12">
											<h3 class="m-0 p-3">Amphibians, Reptiles &#038; Aquatics</h3>
										</div>
									</div>
									<div class="row align-items-stretch">
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 slide-img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Parallax-Image-6.jpg); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:cover; background-position:center center;">
											<!-- <img decoding="async" src=""> -->
										</div>
										<div class="col-12 col-md-6 p-3 p-md-4 slide-content d-flex flex-column align-items-start">
											<p>Ireland’s only native reptile, the viviparous lizard, is versatile in its habitat and can be found in woodlands, marshes, heaths and bogs. Ranging in length from 10–16 cm, they have long bodies, short legs and a long tapering tail. The smooth newt is also native to Ireland and can be found in woodlands, hedgerows, marshes and areas dominated by tussock grassland. They are the only amphibian found in Ireland that has a tail. Atlantic salmon is native to Ireland and is found in most of our rivers.</p>
										</div>
									</div>

								</li>

																							</ul>
						</div>
						<div class="slideup-infobox-header accordion-header" id="heading2">
							<div class="header-image" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/94cc136ba0162897363674c93e5e8856-scaled.jpg);"></div>
							<button class="slide-button collapsed sb-2 px-4 pt-2" type="button" data-counter="2" data-target="#collapse2" aria-expanded="true" aria-controls="collapse2">
								Species							</button>
						</div>
					</li>
												</ul>
	</div>
</section>

															</ul>
			</div>
			<div class="col-12 col-md-6 py-5 px-3 px-md-5 d-flex justify-content-center align-content-center wow fadeInRight">
				<div class="all-links-image-ww d-flex flex-column justify-content-center">
					<div class="all-links-image-wrap my-5 my-md-0 p-4">
											<img decoding="async" class="img-fluid" src="https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/02/Group-8.png" alt="Girl holding Pine Cones">
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</section>

	<div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="trailModalLabel-0" class="modal fade" id="trailModal-0" tabindex="-1">
			<div class="modal-dialog modal-dialog-centered modal-xl">
				<div class="modal-content bg-color-tea color-eden" style="border-radius:0;">
					<div class="modal-body px-2 px-xl-5 py-3 py-md-3">
						<div class="container-fluid px-0 px-md-3">
							<div class="row">
								<div class="col-12 py-4">
									<h2><span class="h6 font-small font-family-gibson-300 color-eden d-block text-center text-uppercase">06 / Learn</span></h2>
								</div>
								<div class="col-12 d-flex modaltrailtitle">
									<button aria-label="Close" class="close circle-arrow-wrap" data-bs-dismiss="modal" type="button">
										<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
									</button>
									<span class="align-self-end pb-3 h1 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium ms-4">Biodiversity</span>
								</div>
								<div class="col-12">
																			<div class="row">
											<div class="col-12 py-4 my-3 h2 color-black">
												<div>Biodiversity refers to the variety of living species on Earth, including plants, animals, bacteria, and fungi. While Earth’s biodiversity is so rich that many species have yet to be discovered, many species are being threatened with extinction due to human activities, putting the Earth’s magnificent biodiversity at risk.</div>
											</div>
											<div class="col-12">
												<img decoding="async" class="img-fluid" src="https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Group-8-1.png">
											</div>
										</div>
																	</div>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
		<div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="trailModalLabel-1" class="modal fade" id="trailModal-1" tabindex="-1">
			<div class="modal-dialog modal-dialog-centered modal-xl">
				<div class="modal-content bg-color-tea color-eden" style="border-radius:0;">
					<div class="modal-body px-2 px-xl-5 py-3 py-md-3">
						<div class="container-fluid px-0 px-md-3">
							<div class="row">
								<div class="col-12 py-4">
									<h2><span class="h6 font-small font-family-gibson-300 color-eden d-block text-center text-uppercase">06 / Learn</span></h2>
								</div>
								<div class="col-12 d-flex modaltrailtitle">
									<button aria-label="Close" class="close circle-arrow-wrap" data-bs-dismiss="modal" type="button">
										<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
									</button>
									<span class="align-self-end pb-3 h1 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium ms-4">Seasonal Tips</span>
								</div>
								<div class="col-12">
										
										<div class="row align-items-stretch">
																																	<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 d-flex flex-column season-wrap">
													<div id="spring-slider" class="carousel slide season-slider flex-grow-1" data-bs-interval="false" data-bs-ride="false" data-bs-wrap="false">
  														<div class="carousel-inner h-100">	 
																																													<div class="carousel-item active h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Rectangle-113.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">What to do when you find a baby bird?</h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>If you encounter a baby bird out in the open, moving it to a safer location with some cover may help its chances. If the chick is very young (with few or no feathers) and you know where the nest is, the best thing to do is to pop the chick back in and let the parents continue to care for it. Do not stay in the vicinity as you may frighten off the adult birds and cause unnecessary stress to the chicks.</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																															<div class="carousel-item  h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/05/Rectangle-137.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">Rewild your garden</h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>Introduce small flower species to your lawn by letting your grass grow between April and July. Encourage native plants to grow wild in your garden, such as  birds-foot-trefoil, foxglove, and many trees and shrubs, such as willow and ivy. Don’t use pesticides. If you have a healthy ecosystem of insects visiting your garden it should keep the natural balance. Turn your garden lights off. Lighting at night disrupts the natural life cycles and rhythms of huge numbers of wildlife, including moths, bats and birds.</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																														
														</div>
													</div>
													<div class="season-title d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center p-3">
														<h3 class="m-0 color-black">Spring</h3>
														<div class="slider-nav-wrap">
															<button class="carousel-control-prev" type="button" data-bs-target="#spring-slider" data-bs-slide="prev">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Previous</span>
															</button>
															<button class="carousel-control-next" type="button" data-bs-target="#spring-slider" data-bs-slide="next">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Next</span>
															</button>
														</div>	
													</div>
												</div>
																							<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 d-flex flex-column season-wrap">
													<div id="summer-slider" class="carousel slide season-slider flex-grow-1" data-bs-interval="false" data-bs-ride="false" data-bs-wrap="false">
  														<div class="carousel-inner h-100">	 
																																													<div class="carousel-item active h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Rectangle-88-1.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">Best plants to attract bees to your garden</h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>Bees are especially drawn to blue, white, yellow, and purple flowers. You also want to prioritise growing single flowers (which have a simple corolla with just one ring of petals) over double flowers (which, like ornamental roses, have multiple layers of petals). Single flowers contain more pollen and nectar than double flowers, and they’re less dense, making it easier for bees to reach the pollen. And don&#8217;t forget to choose native plants with a variety of flower shapes in order to attract many wild bee species.</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																														
														</div>
													</div>
													<div class="season-title d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center p-3">
														<h3 class="m-0 color-black">Summer</h3>
														<div class="slider-nav-wrap">
															<button class="carousel-control-prev" type="button" data-bs-target="#summer-slider" data-bs-slide="prev">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Previous</span>
															</button>
															<button class="carousel-control-next" type="button" data-bs-target="#summer-slider" data-bs-slide="next">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Next</span>
															</button>
														</div>	
													</div>
												</div>
																							<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 d-flex flex-column season-wrap">
													<div id="autumn-slider" class="carousel slide season-slider flex-grow-1" data-bs-interval="false" data-bs-ride="false" data-bs-wrap="false">
  														<div class="carousel-inner h-100">	 
																																													<div class="carousel-item active h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Rectangle-114.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">How to grow saplings from acorns</h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>If you have the space, planting a native oak in your garden is one of the best things you can do for wildlife. Collect acorns that have fallen as these acorns are mature. Viable acorns will sink in a bowl of water and damaged/empty ones will float. Use pots about 3.5 inches deep. Fill the pots with potting mix. Place two acorns sideways in each pot about one inch deep. Water them until water runs out of the drainage holes. Keep the soil moist until the onset of winter weather.</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																															<div class="carousel-item  h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/05/Rectangle-142.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">Time to start planting your bulbs</h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>Now is the time to plant for a colourful spring display. If you choose the right species, you will also help pollinators by supplying nectar-rich flowers when food is in short supply in early spring. Crocuses and grape hyacinths are loved by bees. Snowdrops and winter aconites flower even earlier. If you plant bluebells, make sure that you plant native Irish bluebells rather than invasive Spanish bluebells. Daffodils also attract insects, but avoid the double flowers. Single flowers are always best for pollinators.</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																														
														</div>
													</div>
													<div class="season-title d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center p-3">
														<h3 class="m-0 color-black">Autumn</h3>
														<div class="slider-nav-wrap">
															<button class="carousel-control-prev" type="button" data-bs-target="#autumn-slider" data-bs-slide="prev">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Previous</span>
															</button>
															<button class="carousel-control-next" type="button" data-bs-target="#autumn-slider" data-bs-slide="next">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Next</span>
															</button>
														</div>	
													</div>
												</div>
																							<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 d-flex flex-column season-wrap">
													<div id="winter-slider" class="carousel slide season-slider flex-grow-1" data-bs-interval="false" data-bs-ride="false" data-bs-wrap="false">
  														<div class="carousel-inner h-100">	 
																																													<div class="carousel-item active h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/04/Rectangle-115.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">Winter hiking tips from our park guides</h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>Winter hikes are some of the best. The air is fresh, many trees take on a beautiful new appearance, fewer flies are around and the exercise helps keep the winter blues away. Some precautions are as follows— Check the weather: before heading out, take a minute to make sure the weather isn’t too hostile for walkers. Aim to walk during the day. Wear the right gear: wear numerous small base layers instead of one thick one, boots, hat, gloves and scarf. Alway tell someone where you plan to walk, and don’t forget to hydrate!</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																															<div class="carousel-item  h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/05/Rectangle-143.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">Best bird seed mixes for winter</h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>The time of year that the birds really need you to put out food in your garden is during the winter months (roughly November–March). During these months the natural food sources of fruit, berries, nuts and seeds have been depleted, and there are few invertebrates active and available. The golden rule is that a diversity of food types will give you a diversity of bird species. Peanuts and sunflower hearts are both great options as they’re both eaten by a variety of species, have high protein and calorie contents, and are readily available in most shops.</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																															<div class="carousel-item  h-100 bg-color-tea" >
																	<div class="row">
																		<div class="season-content-img col-12 col-xl-5" role="img" style="background-image:url(https://www.nationalparks.ie/app/uploads/2022/05/Rectangle-144.png); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-position:center center; background-size:cover;"></div>
																		<div class="season-content-text col-12 col-xl-7 p-3 d-flex flex-column justify-content-between">
																			<h3 class="mb-4 color-black font-family-naisiuntamedium font-weight-bold">Get to know your firs, pines and spruces </h3>
																			<div class="color-black m-0"><p>Get to know your tree types to find your perfect Christmas match. Nordman Fir is the nation’s favourite. They have a beautiful shape, wonderful smell, and excellent needle retention. Noble Fir retains its needles very well. This fir has attractive grey-green needles that tilt upwards with very strong branches, (a good choice for heavy decorations.) Scots pine is slightly less common but is a native tree with bright, blue-green foliage and a lovely fragrance. Norway spruce has short, bright green, pointed needles that drop quickly. It has a lovely scent but needs regular watering.</p>
</div>
																		</div>

																	</div>
																</div>	
																														
														</div>
													</div>
													<div class="season-title d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center p-3">
														<h3 class="m-0 color-black">Winter</h3>
														<div class="slider-nav-wrap">
															<button class="carousel-control-prev" type="button" data-bs-target="#winter-slider" data-bs-slide="prev">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Previous</span>
															</button>
															<button class="carousel-control-next" type="button" data-bs-target="#winter-slider" data-bs-slide="next">
																<span class="circle-arrow bg-color-tea"></span>
																<span class="visually-hidden">Next</span>
															</button>
														</div>

											<div class="modal-text-content font-family-naisiuntamedium ps-0 ps-lg-5 pe-0 pb-0">
												<div class="color-black font-weight-bold"><p>The National Parks run lots of nature inspired workshops and events throughout the year. With a strong focus on conservation, biodiversity and sustainability, there are events tailored to all ages and interests.<br />
Family focused events include: guided walks, nature trails, nature-inspired art competitions, insect hunts, birdspotting challenges, and many more. Guided walks and hikes take place across all the parks, in addition to park-specific events such as stargazing in Wild Nephin, and regular nature based walks, talks and workshops in Glenveagh.</p>

	$(document).ready(function() {			
    //play - pause hero carousel
    $(".is-control-playpause a").click(function( event ) {
			event.preventDefault();
      var clicked = $(this);
      if(!clicked.hasClass('active')){
        $(".is-control-playpause a").removeClass('active');
        clicked.addClass('active');
        if(clicked.hasClass('carousel-control-play')){
          $("#heroBannerSlider").carousel("cycle");
        } else { 
          $("#heroBannerSlider").carousel("pause");
        }
      }
		});

    //play - pause hero carousel
    $(".is-time-playpause a").click(function( event ) {
			event.preventDefault();
      var clicked = $(this);
      if(!clicked.hasClass('active')){
        $(".is-time-playpause a").removeClass('active');
        clicked.addClass('active');
        if(clicked.hasClass('time-control-play')){
          timeclock = setInterval(windTheClock, 1000);
        } else { 
          clearInterval(timeclock);
        }
      }
		});

		//skiptomaincontent
		$(".skipcontent").click(function( event ) {
			event.preventDefault();
			$("section:nth-child(6)").attr("tabindex",-1).focus();
			$('html, body').animate({
				scrollTop: $("section:nth-child(6)").offset().top + -75
			}, 100);
		});
		
		//add animation to the main navigation
		$('.dropdown').on('show.bs.dropdown', function() {
			$(this).children('.dropdown-menu').addClass('animated');
		});
		$('.dropdown').on('hide.bs.dropdown', function() {
			$(this).children('.dropdown-menu').removeClass('animated');
		});	

		var swipers = [];
		$('.swiper').each(function(e,i){
		var currid = $(this).data("swp");
		var swpnum = $(this).data("swpnum");
		var swpname = ".swiper_"+currid;
		var swpnext = ".sb_"+currid+"_next";
		var swpprev = ".sb_"+currid+"_prev";
		var options = {
			// Optional parameters
			loop: true,
			// Navigation arrows
			navigation: {
				nextEl: swpnext,
				prevEl: swpprev,
			},
		}
		switch(swpnum){
			case 4:
				options.slidesPerView = 4;
				options.spaceBetween = 60;
				options.breakpoints = {
					320: {
						slidesPerView: 1,
						spaceBetween: 0,
					},
					768: {
						slidesPerView: 3,	
						spaceBetween: 20,
					},
					1024: {
						slidesPerView: 4,
						spaceBetween: 60,
					},
				}
				break; 
			case 3: 
				options.slidesPerView = 3;
				options.spaceBetween = 60;
				options.breakpoints = {
					320: {
						slidesPerView: 1,
						spaceBetween: 0,
					},
					768: {
						slidesPerView: 2,	
						spaceBetween: 20,
					},
					1024: {
						slidesPerView: 3,
						spaceBetween: 60,
					},
				}
				break;
			case 2: 
				options.slidesPerView = 2;
				options.spaceBetween = 60;
				options.breakpoints = {
					320: {
						slidesPerView: 1,
						spaceBetween: 0,
					},
					768: {
						slidesPerView: 2,	
						spaceBetween: 20,
					},
					1024: {
						slidesPerView: 2,
						spaceBetween: 60,
					},
				}
				break;	
			case 1: 
				options.slidesPerView = 1;
				options.spaceBetween = 0;
				break;
			default:
				options.slidesPerView = 4;
				options.spaceBetween = 60;
				options.breakpoints = {
					320: {
						slidesPerView: 1,
						spaceBetween: 0,
					},
					768: {
						slidesPerView: 3,	
						spaceBetween: 20,
					},
					1024: {
						slidesPerView: 4,
						spaceBetween: 60,
					},
				}
		}
		console.log(options);
		swipers[i] = new Swiper(swpname, options);
		
		});

		$(document.body).on('click', '.slide-button', function(){
			var currentbtn = $(this);
			var samebtn = '.sb-'+currentbtn.data('counter');
			var btntarget = currentbtn.data('target');
			
			if($(samebtn).hasClass('collapsed')) { 
				$(samebtn).removeClass('collapsed');
				$(btntarget).addClass('open');
			} else {
				$(samebtn).addClass('collapsed');
				$(btntarget).removeClass('open');
			}
		});

		if(scroll >= 80){
			$('.navbar').addClass('on-scroll');
		}else if(scroll < 80){
			$('.navbar').removeClass('on-scroll');
		}
		$(window).scroll(function (event) {
			var scroll = $(window).scrollTop();
			if(scroll >= 80){
				$('.navbar').addClass('on-scroll');
			}else if(scroll < 80){
				$('.navbar').removeClass('on-scroll');
			}
		});
		
		//if nav open
		jQuery(".navbar-toggler").on("click",function(){
			if (jQuery(".navbar-toggler").attr("aria-expanded") == "false") {
				// Closed				
				jQuery("body").removeClass("on-nav-open");
				jQuery("nav.navbar").slideUp();
				jQuery(".section-hero .navbar-toggler").focus();
				//jQuery("nav.navbar").removeClass("bg-color-light-green");
				//jQuery("nav.navbar").addClass("bg-color-cyprus");
			} else {
				//open
				jQuery("body").addClass("on-nav-open");

		var height = jQuery(".section-menu").outerHeight(); //gets height of header

		jQuery(window).scroll(function(){
			if(jQuery(window).scrollTop() > (topofDiv + height)){

		//function if is on viewport
		$.fn.isInViewport = function() {
			var elementTop = $(this).offset().top;
			var elementBottom = elementTop + $(this).outerHeight();

			var viewportTop = $(window).scrollTop();
			var viewportBottom = viewportTop + $(window).height();

			return elementBottom > viewportTop && elementTop < viewportBottom;
		};

		//rotate temperatures All Parks homepage
		if($(".section-menu .weather-box-item").length > 0){
			$(".weather-box-item").hide();
			$(".weather-box-item:nth-child(1)").fadeIn();

      var places = ['donegal','clare','galway','kerry','wicklow','mayo'];
      for(var i=0; i<places.length;i++){
        getweather(places[i]);
      }
			
      function getweather(pplace){
        var request = jQuery.ajax({
          url: "https://www.nationalparks.ie/weather-output/"+pplace+".php",
          type: "GET",
          dataType: "json"
        });
        request.done(function(data) {
          $(".is-"+pplace+"-weather .weather-box-temperature").html(data.temperature+" °C");
          $(".is-"+pplace+"-weather .weather-description").html(data.weatherDescription);
          $(".is-"+pplace+"-weather .weather-icon").attr("src", "/app/themes/NationalParks/resources/assets/images/weather_icons/light_icons/"+data.symbol+".png");
          $(".is-"+pplace+"-weather .weather-icon-dark").attr("src", "/app/themes/NationalParks/resources/assets/images/weather_icons/dark_icons/"+data.symbol+".png");
        });
      }

			var tempDiv = 1;
			function randomTemperature () {
				tempDiv++;
				if(tempDiv <= 6){
					$(".weather-box-item").hide();
					$(".weather-box-item:nth-child("+tempDiv+")").fadeIn();
				}
				if(tempDiv == 7){
					$(".weather-box-item").hide();
					$(".weather-box-item:nth-child(1)").fadeIn();	
					tempDiv = 1;					
				}
			}

			$(function () {
        //randomTemperature();
				setInterval(randomTemperature, 5000);
			});
		
		};
		
		//on hover change images on All Parks Explore NP Block
		$(".section-park-map-table-row").hover(function() {
			var url = $(this).attr("data-row-url");
			//console.log(url);
			$(".section-park-map-table-images img").each(function(){
				if ( $(this).attr('data-image-url') == url ) {
					$(this).show();
				} else {
					$(this).hide();
				}
			});
		});
		$(".explore-svg-map svg a").hover(function() {
			var url = $(this).attr("href");
			var url2 = url.replace('https://'+document.domain, '');
			//console.log(url2);
			$(".section-park-map-table-images img").each(function(){
				if ( $(this).attr('data-image-url') == url || $(this).attr('data-image-url') == url2 ) {
					$(this).show();
				} else {
					$(this).hide();
				}
			});
		});
		
		//ON SCROLL ANIMATION
		
		
		window.onscroll = function () {
			scrollBarIndicator();
			scrollRotate();
		};
		
		// When the user scrolls the page, increase progress bar 
		function scrollBarIndicator() {
			var winScroll = document.body.scrollTop || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
			var height = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - document.documentElement.clientHeight;
			var scrolled = (winScroll / height) * 100;
			document.getElementById("myBar").style.width = scrolled + "%";
		};

		//rotate compass
		function scrollRotate() {
			// Calculate the degrees of rotation based on the scrolled portion,
			 // the height of the visible part, and height of the whole document
			 var $sqr = $('#national-parks .section-card-extra-element'),
				  $body = $(document.body),
				  bodyHeight = $body.height(),
				  windowHeight = $(window).height();
			 var top = document.documentElement.scrollTop || document.body.scrollTop,
				 rot = (top / 50);
	 
			 // Check if overshooting a full rotation
			 //if (rot > 360) {
				//rot = 360;
			 //}
	 
			 // Rotate the box
			 $sqr.css({ 'transform': 'rotate(' + rot.toFixed() + 'deg)' });
			 //$sqr.html('<div>' + rot.toFixed() + ' &deg;</div>');
			 
			 //console.log(rot);
		}
		
		//on resize get subnav height and add to margin
		var win = $(window); //this = window
		if (win.width() >= 992) { 
			var parkNavHeight = $(".park-subnav").outerHeight();
			$(".section-park-anchor-navigation").css("margin-bottom", parkNavHeight+"px");
			$(window).on('resize', function(){
				windowWidth = $(this); //this = window
				if (windowWidth.width() >= 992) {
					parkNavHeight = $(".park-subnav").outerHeight();
					$(".section-park-anchor-navigation").css("margin-bottom", parkNavHeight+"px");
				};
			});
		};	
		
		//anchor link ofset
		$(document).on('click', 'a:not(.btn-backtop):not(.skipcontent):not(.carousel-controls)[href^="#"]', function (event) {
			event.preventDefault();
			$('html, body').animate({
				scrollTop: $($.attr(this, 'href')).offset().top + -75
			}, 0);
		});		
	});
</script>

	<script>
		
		//disable/enable scroll
		$.fn.disableScroll = function() {
			window.oldScrollPos = $(window).scrollTop();

			$(window).on('scroll.scrolldisabler',function ( event ) {
			   $(window).scrollTop( window.oldScrollPos );
			   event.preventDefault();
			});
		};
		$.fn.enableScroll = function() {
			$(window).off('scroll.scrolldisabler');
		};
		$("body").disableScroll();
		
		
		
		//loader counter
		$(document).ready(function() {	
			$('#loading-panel .progress-bar').each(function() {
			  var $this = $(this),
				  countTo = $this.attr('aria-valuemax');
			  
			  //aria-valuenow
			  $({ countNum: $this.attr("aria-valuenow")}).animate({
				countNum: countTo
			  },

			  {

				duration: 600,
				easing:'linear',
				step: function() {
				  $this.attr("aria-valuenow", Math.floor(this.countNum));
				},
				complete: function() {
				  $this.attr("aria-valuenow", this.countNum);
				}
			  });

			  //text
			  $({ countNum: $this.text()}).animate({
				countNum: countTo
			  },

			  {

				duration: 600,
				easing:'linear',
				step: function() {
				  $this.text(Math.floor(this.countNum)+"%");
				},
				complete: function() {
				  $this.text(this.countNum+"%");
				}
			  });
			  
			  //css width
			  $({ countNum: $this.css("width")}).animate({
				countNum: countTo
			  },

			  {

				duration: 600,
				easing:'linear',
				step: function() {
				  $this.css("width", Math.floor(this.countNum)+"%");
				},
				complete: function() {
				  $this.css("width", this.countNum+"%");
				}
			  });		  
		  
		  
		  
			});
		});
	
		$(window).on("load", function () {
			setTimeout(function(){
				$('#loading-panel .progress-bar').text("100%");
				$('#loading-panel .progress-bar').css("width", "100%");
			}, 1300);
			setTimeout(function(){
				$(".loading-icon").fadeOut();
				$("#loading-panel-top").animate({
					top: '-2000px'
				}, 2000);
			}, 2000);
			setTimeout(function(){
				$("#loading-panel-bottom").animate({
					bottom: '-200px'
				}, 1000);
			}, 3000);
			setTimeout(function(){
				//$(".section-hero").addClass("wow fadeInDown");
				//$(".section-menu").addClass("wow fadeInUp");
				$("#loading-panel, .loading-logo").hide();
				$('.loading-logo').css("z-index", "0");
				$('.loading-logo').fadeOut();
				$("body").enableScroll();
			}, 4000);
			
		});
		
		//clone hero banner caption
		var heroBannerCaption = jQuery(".carousel-item.active .carousel-caption h2").html();
		jQuery(".carousel-indicators .caption").html(heroBannerCaption);
		$('#heroBannerSlider').on('slid.bs.carousel', function () {
			var heroBannerCaption = jQuery(".carousel-item.active .carousel-caption h2").html();
			jQuery(".carousel-indicators .caption").html(heroBannerCaption);
		});
		
	</script>
